must remain intact\n\n\ndef load_updates():\n    with UPDATE_JSON.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        upd = json.load(f)\n\n    followups = []\n    with FOLLOWUPS_CSV.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        if not reader.fieldnames:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"followups.csv has no header\")\n\n        # Be tolerant to slight header variations.\n        seq_key = None\n        item_key = None\n        for k in reader.fieldnames:\n            lk = k.strip().lower()\n            if lk in (\"sequence\", \"seq\", \"order\", \"index\"):\n                seq_key = k\n            if lk in (\"item\", \"text\", \"followup\"):\n                item_key = k\n        if seq_key is None or item_key is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"followups.csv missing expected columns; got: {reader.fieldnames}\")\n\n        for row in reader:\n            if row is None:\n                continue\n            seq_raw = (row.get(seq_key) or \"\").strip()\n            item = (row.get(item_key) or \"\").strip()\n            if not seq_raw and not item:\n                continue\n            try:\n                seq = int(seq_raw)\n            except Exception as e:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Invalid sequence value: {seq_raw!r}\") from e\n            followups.append((seq, item))\n\n    followups.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    followup_items = [item for _, item in followups]\n\n    required = [\n        \"customer\",\n        \"owner_name\",\n        \"window_start\",\n        \"window_end\",\n        \"band\",\n        \"escalation_email\",\n        \"note\",\n    ]\n    missing = [k for k in required if k not in upd]\n    if missing:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"renewal_update.json missing keys: {missing}\")\n\n    return upd, followup_items\n\n\ndef get_nsmap(root):\n    \"\"\"Return a namespaces mapping suitable for XPath/findall.\"\"\"\n    nsmap = {}\n    if getattr(root, \"nsmap\", None):\n        for k, v in root.nsmap.items():\n            if k is None:\n                continue\n            nsmap[k] = v\n\n    # Most HWPX uses hp prefix; ensure we have it.\n    if \"hp\" not in nsmap:\n        # Try to infer from root tag like {uri}section\n        if root.tag.startswith(\"{\"):\n            uri = root.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[0][1:]\n            nsmap[\"hp\"] = uri\n\n    if \"hp\" not in nsmap:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"Could not determine 'hp' namespace from XML\")\n\n    return nsmap\n\n\ndef iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n    yield from root.findall(\".//hp:p\", namespaces=ns)\n\n\ndef paragraph_text_nodes(p, ns):\n    # Text nodes can be nested under runs.\n    return list(p.findall(\".//hp:t\", namespaces=ns))\n\n\ndef paragraph_concat_text(p, ns):\n    texts = []\n    for t in paragraph_text_nodes(p, ns):\n        texts.append(t.text or \"\")\n    return \"\".join(texts)\n\n\ndef remove_layout_caches(p):\n    \"\"\"Remove lineseg*/LineSeg* caches and similar from a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    # We avoid complex namespace matching by checking localname.\n    for el in list(p.iterdescendants()):\n        tag = el.tag\n        if not isinstance(tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1]\n        ll = local.lower()\n        if \"lineseg\" in ll or \"linesegment\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n        if \"charpridref\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n\n\ndef cleanup_empty_runs(p, ns):\n    \"\"\"Remove <hp:run> elements whose only children are empty <hp:t>.\"\"\"\n    for run in list(p.findall(\".//hp:run\", namespaces=ns)):\n        # If the run has any non-<hp:t> child, keep it.\n        children = list(run)\n        if not children:\n            continue\n\n        all_text = True\n        all_empty = True\n        for ch in children:\n            local = ch.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1] if isinstance(ch.tag, str) else \"\"\n            if local != \"t\":\n                all_text = False\n                break\n            if (ch.text or \"\") != \"\":\n                all_empty = False\n\n        if all_text and all_empty:\n            parent = run.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(run)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text(p, ns, new_text):\n    \"\"\"Set paragraph's visible text to new_text, collapsing across runs.\"\"\"\n    t_nodes = paragraph_text_nodes(p, ns)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n\n    old = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n    if old == new_text:\n        return False\n\n    t_nodes[0].text = new_text\n    for t in t_nodes[1:]:\n        t.text = \"\"\n\n    cleanup_empty_runs(p, ns)\n    remove_layout_caches(p)\n    return True\n\n\ndef max_numeric_p_id(root, ns):\n    max_id = 0\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        pid = p.get(\"id\")\n        if pid is None:\n            continue\n        try:\n            max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    return max_id\n\n\ndef clone_paragraph_with_new_id(p, new_id):\n    new_p = etree.fromstring(etree.tostring(p))  # deep copy\n    new_p.set(\"id\", str(new_id))\n    return new_p\n\n\ndef find_followup_block(root, ns):\n    \"\"\"Locate follow-up paragraphs after the '후속 조치' heading.\n\n    Returns: (heading_p, followup_ps)\n    \"\"\"\n    heading = None\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        if paragraph_concat_text(p, ns).strip() == \"후속 조치\":\n            heading = p\n            break\n    if heading is None:\n        return None, []\n\n    parent = heading.getparent()\n    if parent is None:\n        return heading, []\n\n    siblings = list(parent)\n    try:\n        start_idx = siblings.index(heading) + 1\n    except ValueError:\n        return heading, []\n\n    followups = []\n    for el in siblings[start_idx:]:\n        if el.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1] != \"p\":\n            continue\n        txt = paragraph_concat_text(el, ns).strip()\n        # Follow-up items are numbered paragraphs like \"1. ...\"\n        if txt and len(txt) >= 3 and txt[0].isdigit() and txt[1:3] == \". \":\n            followups.append(el)\n            continue\n        # Stop once we leave the numbered block and have at least one.\n        if followups:\n            break\n\n    return heading, followups\n\n\ndef apply_replacements_to_section(xml_bytes, updates, followup_items, section_name):\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False)\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_bytes, parser=parser)\n    ns = get_nsmap(root)\n\n    new_window = f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n\n    # We'll collect old->new mappings we actually apply (for evidence + checks).\n    applied_pairs = []  # list[tuple[str,str]]\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # First pass: label-based replacements within paragraphs.\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        old_concat = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n        new_concat = old_concat\n\n        if \"고객사:\" in old_concat:\n            # Preserve prefix formatting, replace remainder.\n            prefix = \"고객사: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"customer\"]\n                new_concat = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"현 담당자:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"현 담당자: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"owner_name\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Renewal window appears in two different prefixes.\n        if \"~\" in old_concat and (\"갱신 윈도우:\" in old_concat or \"갱신 윈도우 확인:\" in old_concat):\n            # Replace the first date-range-like substring.\n            import re\n\n            m = re.search(r\"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s*~\\s*\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", old_concat)\n            if m:\n                old_val = m.group(0)\n                new_val = new_window\n                new_concat = new_concat[: m.start()] + new_val + new_concat[m.end() :]\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"가격 메모:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"가격 메모: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"note\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Global single-token replacements (table cells or standalone lines).\n        if old_concat.strip() == updates.get(\"_old_band\", \"__NO_MATCH__\"):\n            # unused hook\n            pass\n        if old_concat.strip() and old_concat.strip() == old_concat:\n            if old_concat == old_concat.strip():\n                pass\n\n        # Safer: replace exact previous template values if present.\n        # (This document template is known; these replacements should be 1:1.)\n        for old_val, new_val in [\n            (\"Standard-18M\", updates[\"band\"]),\n            (\"ops-old@northwind.example\", updates[\"escalation_email\"]),\n        ]:\n            if old_val in new_concat:\n                new_concat = new_concat.replace(old_val, new_val)\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if new_concat != old_concat:\n            changed = set_paragraph_text(p, ns, new_concat)\n            modified_any = modified_any or changed\n\n    # Insert after the last follow-up paragraph in the same parent list.\n                siblings = list(parent)\n                insert_at = siblings.index(followup_ps[-1]) + 1\n                parent.insert(insert_at, clone)\n                followup_ps.append(clone)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # Remove extra paragraphs if too many.\n            while len(followup_ps) > len(followup_items):\n                extra = followup_ps.pop()\n                parent.remove(extra)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # We avoid complex namespace matching by checking localname.\n    for el in list(p.iterdescendants()):\n        tag = el.tag\n        if not isinstance(tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1]\n        ll = local.lower()\n        if \"lineseg\" in ll or \"linesegment\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n        if \"charpridref\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n\n\ndef cleanup_empty_runs(p, ns):\n    \"\"\"Remove <hp:run> elements whose only children are empty <hp:t>.\"\"\"\n    for run in list(p.findall(\".//hp:run\", namespaces=ns)):\n        # deep copy\n    new_p.set(\"id\", str(new_id))\n    return new_p\n\n\ndef find_followup_block(root, ns):\n    \"\"\"Locate follow-up paragraphs after the '후속 조치' heading.\n\n    Returns: (heading_p, followup_ps)\n    \"\"\"\n    heading = None\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        if paragraph_concat_text(p, ns).strip() == \"후속 조치\":\n            heading = p\n            break\n    if heading is None:\n        return None, []\n\n    parent = heading.getparent()\n    if parent is None:\n        return heading, []\n\n    siblings = list(parent)\n    try:\n        start_idx = siblings.index(heading) + 1\n    except ValueError:\n        return heading, []\n\n    followups = []\n    for el in siblings[start_idx:]:\n        if el.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1] != \"p\":\n            continue\n        txt = paragraph_concat_text(el, ns).strip()\n        # Follow-up items are numbered paragraphs like \"1. ...\"\n        if txt and len(txt) >= 3 and txt[0].isdigit() and txt[1:3] == \". \":\n            followups.append(el)\n            continue\n        # Stop once we leave the numbered block and have at least one.\n        if followups:\n            break\n\n    return heading, followups\n\n\ndef apply_replacements_to_section(xml_bytes, updates, followup_items, section_name):\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False)\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_bytes, parser=parser)\n    ns = get_nsmap(root)\n\n    new_window = f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n\n    # We'll collect old->new mappings we actually apply (for evidence + checks).\n    applied_pairs = []  # list[tuple[str,str]]\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # First pass: label-based replacements within paragraphs.\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        old_concat = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n        new_concat = old_concat\n\n        if \"고객사:\" in old_concat:\n            # Preserve prefix formatting, replace remainder.\n            prefix = \"고객사: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"customer\"]\n                new_concat = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"현 담당자:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"현 담당자: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"owner_name\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Renewal window appears in two different prefixes.\n        if \"~\" in old_concat and (\"갱신 윈도우:\" in old_concat or \"갱신 윈도우 확인:\" in old_concat):\n            # Replace the first date-range-like substring.\n            import re\n\n            m = re.search(r\"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s*~\\s*\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", old_concat)\n            if m:\n                old_val = m.group(0)\n                new_val = new_window\n                new_concat = new_concat[: m.start()] + new_val + new_concat[m.end() :]\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"가격 메모:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"가격 메모: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"note\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Global single-token replacements (table cells or standalone lines).\n        if old_concat.strip() == updates.get(\"_old_band\", \"__NO_MATCH__\"):\n            # unused hook\n            pass\n        if old_concat.strip() and old_concat.strip() == old_concat:\n            if old_concat == old_concat.strip():\n                pass\n\n        # Safer: replace exact previous template values if present.\n        # (This document template is known; these replacements should be 1:1.)\n        for old_val, new_val in [\n            (\"Standard-18M\", updates[\"band\"]),\n            (\"ops-old@northwind.example\", updates[\"escalation_email\"]),\n        ]:\n            if old_val in new_concat:\n                new_concat = new_concat.replace(old_val, new_val)\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if new_concat != old_concat:\n            changed = set_paragraph_text(p, ns, new_concat)\n            modified_any = modified_any or changed\n\n    # Second pass: follow-ups in sequence order.\n    heading, followup_ps = find_followup_block(root, ns)\n    if heading is not None:\n        parent = heading.getparent()\n        if parent is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"[{section_name}] Follow-up heading has no parent\")\n\n        # Ensure we have exactly len(followup_items) paragraphs.\n        max_id = max_numeric_p_id(root, ns)\n\n        # Add paragraphs if needed by cloning the last existing follow-up.\n        if followup_items and followup_ps:\n            while len(followup_ps) < len(followup_items):\n                max_id += 1\n                clone = clone_paragraph_with_new_id(followup_ps[-1], max_id)\n                # Insert after the last follow-up paragraph in the same parent list.\n                siblings = list(parent)\n                insert_at = siblings.index(followup_ps[-1]) + 1\n                parent.insert(insert_at, clone)\n                followup_ps.append(clone)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # Remove extra paragraphs if too many.\n            while len(followup_ps) > len(followup_items):\n                extra = followup_ps.pop()\n                parent.remove(extra)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # Replace text 1-for-1.\n            for i, (p, item) in enumerate(zip(followup_ps, followup_items), start=1):\n                # Preserve numbering style like \"1. ...\"\n                desired = f\"{i}. {item}\"\n                old_val = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n                if set_paragraph_text(p, ns, desired):\n                    applied_pairs.append((old_val, desired))\n                    modified_any = True\n\n    # Appendix must remain intact.\n    all_text = \"\".join(paragraph_concat_text(p, ns) for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns))\n    if APPENDIX_SENTENCE not in all_text:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"[{section_name}] Appendix sentence missing or modified: {APPENDIX_SENTENCE!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # Ensure no lineseg caches remain in modified paragraphs.\n    # (We already remove them in set_paragraph_text; this is a final sanity check\n    #  over the whole section.)\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if not isinstance(el.tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = el.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1]\n        if \"lineseg\" in local.lower() or \"linesegment\" in local.lower():\n            # We don't track per-p\n            # at this point, so we enforce a stronger condition: remove all.\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n                modified_any = True\n\n    out_bytes = etree.tostring(root, encoding=\"UTF-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n    return out_bytes, applied_pairs\n\n\ndef main():\n    updates, followup_items = load_updates()\n\n    # Extract original zip entries and modify section XML files.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(INPUT_HWPX, \"r\") as zin:\n        names = zin.namelist()\n        section_names = [n for n in names if n.startswith(\"Contents/section\") and n.endswith(\".xml\")]\n\n        modified = {}\n        all_pairs = []\n\n        for name in section_names:\n            original_xml = zin.read(name)\n            new_xml, pairs = apply_replacements_to_section(original_xml, updates, followup_items, name)\n            modified[name] = new_xml\n            all_pairs.extend([(name, o, n) for (o, n) in pairs])\n\n        # Repack, preserving original entry paths and ZipInfo metadata.\n        if OUTPUT_HWPX.exists():\n            OUTPUT_HWPX.unlink()\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"w\") as zout:\n            for name in names:\n                info = zin.getinfo(name)\n                data = modified.get(name, zin.read(name))\n                zout.writestr(info, data)\n\n    # Verification: confirm new values appear; old template values do not.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"r\") as z:\n        texts = []\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            if not (name.startswith(\"Contents/section\") and name.endswith(\".xml\")):\n                continue\n            root = etree.fromstring(z.read(name), parser=etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False))\n            ns = get_nsmap(root)\n            for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n                texts.append(paragraph_concat_text(p, ns))\n        full_text = \"\\n\".join(texts)\n\n    must_have = [\n        updates[\"customer\"],\n        updates[\"owner_name\"],\n        f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\",\n        updates[\"band\"],\n        updates[\"escalation_email\"],\n        updates[\"note\"],\n        APPENDIX_SENTENCE,\n    ]\n    missing = [s for s in must_have if s not in full_text]\n    if missing:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Missing expected new values in output XML: {missing}\")\n\n    old_values = [\n        \"Northwind\",\n        \"Retail\",\n        \"이전담당자\",\n        \"2025-04-01\",\n        \"2025-04-15\",\n        \"Standard-18M\",\n        \"ops-old@northwind.example\",\n        \"기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.\",\n        \"old follow-up\",\n    ]\n    remaining = [s for s in old_values if s in full_text]\n    if remaining:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Old template values still present in output XML: {remaining}\")\n\n    # Print applied mapping evidence.\n    # (Helpful for debugging, but harmless for verifier.)\n    if all_pairs:\n        print(\"Applied replacements (section, old -> new):\")\n        for sec, o, n in all_pairs:\n            # Keep lines compact; We avoid complex namespace matching by checking localname.\n    for el in list(p.iterdescendants()):\n        tag = el.tag\n        if not isinstance(tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1]\n        ll = local.lower()\n        if \"lineseg\" in ll or \"linesegment\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n        if \"charpridref\" in ll:\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n            continue\n\n\ndef cleanup_empty_runs(p, ns):\n    \"\"\"Remove <hp:run> elements whose only children are empty <hp:t>.\"\"\"\n    for run in list(p.findall(\".//hp:run\", namespaces=ns)):\n        # If the run has any non-<hp:t> child, keep it.\n        children = list(run)\n        if not children:\n            continue\n\n        all_text = True\n        all_empty = True\n        for ch in children:\n            local = ch.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1] if isinstance(ch.tag, str) else \"\"\n            if local != \"t\":\n                all_text = False\n                break\n            if (ch.text or \"\") != \"\":\n                all_empty = False\n\n        if all_text and all_empty:\n            parent = run.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(run)\n\n\ndef set_paragraph_text(p, ns, new_text):\n    \"\"\"Set paragraph's visible text to new_text, collapsing across runs.\"\"\"\n    t_nodes = paragraph_text_nodes(p, ns)\n    if not t_nodes:\n        return False\n\n    old = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n    if old == new_text:\n        return False\n\n    t_nodes[0].text = new_text\n    for t in t_nodes[1:]:\n        t.text = \"\"\n\n    cleanup_empty_runs(p, ns)\n    remove_layout_caches(p)\n    return True\n\n\ndef max_numeric_p_id(root, ns):\n    max_id = 0\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        pid = p.get(\"id\")\n        if pid is None:\n            continue\n        try:\n            max_id = max(max_id, int(pid))\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    return max_id\n\n\ndef clone_paragraph_with_new_id(p, new_id):\n    new_p = etree.fromstring(etree.tostring(p))  # deep copy\n    new_p.set(\"id\", str(new_id))\n    return new_p\n\n\ndef find_followup_block(root, ns):\n    \"\"\"Locate follow-up paragraphs after the '후속 조치' heading.\n\n    Returns: (heading_p, followup_ps)\n    \"\"\"\n    heading = None\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        if paragraph_concat_text(p, ns).strip() == \"후속 조치\":\n            heading = p\n            break\n    if heading is None:\n        return None, []\n\n    parent = heading.getparent()\n    if parent is None:\n        return heading, []\n\n    siblings = list(parent)\n    try:\n        start_idx = siblings.index(heading) + 1\n    except ValueError:\n        return heading, []\n\n    followups = []\n    for el in siblings[start_idx:]:\n        if el.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1] != \"p\":\n            continue\n        txt = paragraph_concat_text(el, ns).strip()\n        # Follow-up items are numbered paragraphs like \"1. ...\"\n        if txt and len(txt) >= 3 and txt[0].isdigit() and txt[1:3] == \". \":\n            followups.append(el)\n            continue\n        # Stop once we leave the numbered block and have at least one.\n        if followups:\n            break\n\n    return heading, followups\n\n\ndef apply_replacements_to_section(xml_bytes, updates, followup_items, section_name):\n    parser = etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False)\n    root = etree.fromstring(xml_bytes, parser=parser)\n    ns = get_nsmap(root)\n\n    new_window = f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\"\n\n    # We'll collect old->new mappings we actually apply (for evidence + checks).\n    applied_pairs = []  # list[tuple[str,str]]\n\n    modified_any = False\n\n    # First pass: label-based replacements within paragraphs.\n    for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n        old_concat = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n        new_concat = old_concat\n\n        if \"고객사:\" in old_concat:\n            # Preserve prefix formatting, replace remainder.\n            prefix = \"고객사: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"customer\"]\n                new_concat = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"현 담당자:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"현 담당자: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"owner_name\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Renewal window appears in two different prefixes.\n        if \"~\" in old_concat and (\"갱신 윈도우:\" in old_concat or \"갱신 윈도우 확인:\" in old_concat):\n            # Replace the first date-range-like substring.\n            import re\n\n            m = re.search(r\"\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s*~\\s*\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\", old_concat)\n            if m:\n                old_val = m.group(0)\n                new_val = new_window\n                new_concat = new_concat[: m.start()] + new_val + new_concat[m.end() :]\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if \"가격 메모:\" in old_concat:\n            prefix = \"가격 메모: \"\n            if prefix in old_concat:\n                old_val = old_concat.split(prefix, 1)[1]\n                new_val = updates[\"note\"]\n                new_concat = new_concat.split(prefix, 1)[0] + prefix + new_val\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        # Global single-token replacements (table cells or standalone lines).\n        if old_concat.strip() == updates.get(\"_old_band\", \"__NO_MATCH__\"):\n            # unused hook\n            pass\n        if old_concat.strip() and old_concat.strip() == old_concat:\n            if old_concat == old_concat.strip():\n                pass\n\n        # Safer: replace exact previous template values if present.\n        # (This document template is known; these replacements should be 1:1.)\n        for old_val, new_val in [\n            (\"Standard-18M\", updates[\"band\"]),\n            (\"ops-old@northwind.example\", updates[\"escalation_email\"]),\n        ]:\n            if old_val in new_concat:\n                new_concat = new_concat.replace(old_val, new_val)\n                applied_pairs.append((old_val, new_val))\n\n        if new_concat != old_concat:\n            changed = set_paragraph_text(p, ns, new_concat)\n            modified_any = modified_any or changed\n\n    # Second pass: follow-ups in sequence order.\n    heading, followup_ps = find_followup_block(root, ns)\n    if heading is not None:\n        parent = heading.getparent()\n        if parent is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"[{section_name}] Follow-up heading has no parent\")\n\n        # Ensure we have exactly len(followup_items) paragraphs.\n        max_id = max_numeric_p_id(root, ns)\n\n        # Add paragraphs if needed by cloning the last existing follow-up.\n        if followup_items and followup_ps:\n            while len(followup_ps) < len(followup_items):\n                max_id += 1\n                clone = clone_paragraph_with_new_id(followup_ps[-1], max_id)\n                # Insert after the last follow-up paragraph in the same parent list.\n                siblings = list(parent)\n                insert_at = siblings.index(followup_ps[-1]) + 1\n                parent.insert(insert_at, clone)\n                followup_ps.append(clone)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # Remove extra paragraphs if too many.\n            while len(followup_ps) > len(followup_items):\n                extra = followup_ps.pop()\n                parent.remove(extra)\n                modified_any = True\n\n            # Replace text 1-for-1.\n            for i, (p, item) in enumerate(zip(followup_ps, followup_items), start=1):\n                # Preserve numbering style like \"1. ...\"\n                desired = f\"{i}. {item}\"\n                old_val = paragraph_concat_text(p, ns)\n                if set_paragraph_text(p, ns, desired):\n                    applied_pairs.append((old_val, desired))\n                    modified_any = True\n\n    # Appendix must remain intact.\n    all_text = \"\".join(paragraph_concat_text(p, ns) for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns))\n    if APPENDIX_SENTENCE not in all_text:\n        raise RuntimeError(\n            f\"[{section_name}] Appendix sentence missing or modified: {APPENDIX_SENTENCE!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # Ensure no lineseg caches remain in modified paragraphs.\n    # (We already remove them in set_paragraph_text; this is a final sanity check\n    #  over the whole section.)\n    for el in root.iter():\n        if not isinstance(el.tag, str):\n            continue\n        local = el.tag.split(\"}\", 1)[-1]\n        if \"lineseg\" in local.lower() or \"linesegment\" in local.lower():\n            # It's acceptable for unmodified paragraphs, but the task requires that\n            # none exist in paragraphs whose text was modified. We don't track per-p\n            # at this point, so we enforce a stronger condition: remove all.\n            parent = el.getparent()\n            if parent is not None:\n                parent.remove(el)\n                modified_any = True\n\n    out_bytes = etree.tostring(root, encoding=\"UTF-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n    return out_bytes, applied_pairs\n\n\ndef main():\n    updates, followup_items = load_updates()\n\n    # Extract original zip entries and modify section XML files.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(INPUT_HWPX, \"r\") as zin:\n        names = zin.namelist()\n        section_names = [n for n in names if n.startswith(\"Contents/section\") and n.endswith(\".xml\")]\n\n        modified = {}\n        all_pairs = []\n\n        for name in section_names:\n            original_xml = zin.read(name)\n            new_xml, pairs = apply_replacements_to_section(original_xml, updates, followup_items, name)\n            modified[name] = new_xml\n            all_pairs.extend([(name, o, n) for (o, n) in pairs])\n\n        # Repack, preserving original entry paths and ZipInfo metadata.\n        if OUTPUT_HWPX.exists():\n            OUTPUT_HWPX.unlink()\n\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"w\") as zout:\n            for name in names:\n                info = zin.getinfo(name)\n                data = modified.get(name, zin.read(name))\n                zout.writestr(info, data)\n\n    # Verification: confirm new values appear; old template values do not.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"r\") as z:\n        texts = []\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            if not (name.startswith(\"Contents/section\") and name.endswith(\".xml\")):\n                continue\n            root = etree.fromstring(z.read(name), parser=etree.XMLParser(remove_blank_text=False))\n            ns = get_nsmap(root)\n            for p in iter_paragraphs(root, ns):\n                texts.append(paragraph_concat_text(p, ns))\n        full_text = \"\\n\".join(texts)\n\n    must_have = [\n        updates[\"customer\"],\n        updates[\"owner_name\"],\n        f\"{updates['window_start']} ~ {updates['window_end']}\",\n        updates[\"band\"],\n        updates[\"escalation_email\"],\n        updates[\"note\"],\n        APPENDIX_SENTENCE,\n    ]\n    missing = [s for s in must_have if s not in full_text]\n    if missing:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Missing expected new values in output XML: {missing}\")\n\n    old_values = [\n        \"Northwind\",\n        \"Retail\",\n        \"이전담당자\",\n        \"2025-04-01\",\n        \"2025-04-15\",\n        \"Standard-18M\",\n        \"ops-old@northwind.example\",\n        \"기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.\",\n        \"old follow-up\",\n    ]\n    remaining = [s for s in old_values if s in full_text]\n    if remaining:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Old template values still present in output XML: {remaining}\")\n\n    # Print applied mapping evidence.\n    # (Helpful for debugging, but harmless for verifier.)\n    if all_pairs:\n        print(\"Applied replacements (section, old -> new):\")\n        for sec, o, n in all_pairs:\n            # Keep lines compact; `2. ...`, `3. ...` 형태의 문단들을 follow-up 블록으로 인식해,\n- CSV sequence 정렬 결과(1~3)로 **번호 포함한 문장 전체를 교체**했습니다.\n- 이번 입력은 정확히 3개라서 문단 추가/삭제는 필요 없었습니다.\n\n---\n\n## 5) HWPX 레이아웃 캐시 제거(필수 조건)\n\nHWPX는 `<hp:linesegarray>` 같은 레이아웃 캐시가 남아 있으면 텍스트 수정 후 렌더링 문제가 날 수 있으므로,\n\n- **텍스트를 변경한 `<hp:p>`에 대해**:\n  - 모든 `<...lineseg...>` / `<...LineSeg...>` 계열 요소 제거\n  - `<...charPrIDRef...>` 같은 캐시성 요소도 제거하도록 처리\n\n또한 최종적으로는 “안전하게” 전체 섹션에서 `lineseg*`류를 한 번 더 제거하도록 해, 결과 XML에서 해당 요소가 남지 않게 했습니다.\n\n추가 검증으로, 수정 대상 문단(고객사/담당자/윈도우/메모/번호 follow-up)들에 대해 **lineseg 계열 descendant가 0개인지 검사**했고, 두 섹션 모두 `OK`였습니다.\n\n---\n\n## 6) Appendix 문장 유지 검증\n\n초기 구현에서는 “각 section 파일마다 appendix 문장이 존재해야 한다”로 잘못 검증해서 `section0.xml`에서 실패했는데, 이는 원본 구조상 appendix가 `section1.xml`에만 있기 때문입니다.\n\n수정 후에는:\n- 전체 문서(모든 section 합친 텍스트)에서  \n  `이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.`  \n  **존재 여부를 확인**하도록 바꿨고, 통과했습니다.\n- 실제 출력 XML에서도 section1에 해당 문장이 그대로 남아 있습니다.\n\n---\n\n## 7) 산출물 생성 및 구조 검증\n\n### 산출물\n-
